When it matters, carry amount, sign, denomination, unit and state as printed, retaining how each value was derived. Record action status only where evidenced. A speaker’s intention, chart on a page and later resulting balance have different authority. Give directed changes their own rows if reciprocity or disagreement matters. Keep comparisons across distinct episodes and significant form choices directly retrievable.

## Claim and forecast history

A row marked "independent prospect" was frozen in the specialist's own lane. In V01–V05 the specialists saw each volume's front cover and flap before their first freeze, and the reviewer saw them on its first contact sheet; the principals did not see them until after MAIN_EXIT. The [checkpoint errata](checkpoints/FX_KURUMI_CHECKPOINT_ERRATA.md) give every such row's exposure disposition for V01–V04, and the [V05 checkpoint](checkpoints/FX_KURUMI_V05_CHECKPOINT.md#7-exposure-and-contamination-record) for V05. A row whose predictive credit changes records the credit separately from the observed outcome (2026-11-22).

| FXK-V05-C001 | V05's governing thesis: threads of money, concealed information and learned habit bind the four; Mochiko's creed (concealment, dominance, the fair arena, loss-cutting) is given a biography of neglect and humiliation, bound to it by repeated lines ("施し", friends; E061), and meets three tests — Yasuko's claim to reciprocity, the leak of her position through Yasuko to Kurumi, the exposure of her lie in Mebuki's hearing; Mebuki escalates "rescue" shorts the narrator calls "ただのポジション", admits error, cannot realize the loss, resigns; Kurumi brackets a greed-framed long, is paid at 120.05 while away from the screen, finds herself by her own after-tax reckoning still "あと300万円" short, is tempted by "億の世界" and checked by the memory of her father's slap, then opens a 300-lot short sized to the gap, with a take-profit and no stop shown; responsibility refined (Yasuko concedes 自己責任 and claims friendship) (principal, EVALUATION) | Principal's running notes; MAIN_EXIT | i0006–i0053, i0060–i0102, i0129–i0146, i0151–i0162; narrator i0090, i0095; closing image i0162. Against: §D.2 | NEW; EVAL on OBS HIGH and form; revised at reconciliation (reviewer C-01, C-07); V05 reading §D.1 |
| FXK-V05-C002 | Competing thesis: V05 is chiefly a sympathetic origin story; Mochiko's coldness is self-protection; the loan a disclosed hedge Mebuki chose and Yasuko accepts as 自己責任; the café injury accidental; the real danger is Kurumi's grief and restitution drive, which turns a nearly reached target into a larger bet (principal; wording revised at reconciliation, C-19) | Principal's running notes | For: i0018–i0039, i0110–i0126, i0133, i0139. Against: i0048, i0130–i0131, i0138, i0144; her doctrine exempts her own family support (i0028) | NEW; OPEN (live); §D.2, §L |

| FXK-V07-C001 | V07's governing thesis: at Christmas the others move on and disclose (Mebuki's −654.4%, debt and leave; Mochiko's exact +74% and her confession to Mebuki; Yasuko crowned for her comic) while Kurumi's 643.4% is set aside by rule and her only position is the 300-lot long; on 7 January two daughters "cursed by their mothers" disclose, and Mochiko withholds an invitation and a warning; on 15 January the narrator frames the Swiss franc shock as an equal death indifferent to circumstance, while Mochiko's reasoning makes Kurumi's ruin legible as the product of ≈24×, no stop and a gap that turned a forced loss-cut into a shortfall; the black box gives the reader −101,534,644 (−120,284,479) before Kurumi reads any figure; she passes through avoidance, breathlessness and denial to "自己責任" and, by her childhood symmetry inference, to a plan that repeats her mother's 2008 plan (the identical 1,005,144, 400×, "取り返すなんて一瞬", seven doublings), recognised ("お母さんとおんなじ") and adopted; the narrator calls it blindness; Mochiko never speaks (principal, EVALUATION) | Principal's running notes; F01–F06; MAIN_EXIT | i0003–i0150. Against: §D.2 | NEW; EVAL on OBS HIGH and form; "re-enactment" EVAL MOD-HIGH; V07 reading §D.1 |
| FXK-V07-C002 | Competing thesis: V07 is chiefly an exogenous disaster (a central bank breaking its word three days after a public promise; an equal death) and a resilient young woman's acceptance of responsibility; her thesis was official policy, she watched the right risk, a stop might also have slipped in a market with no trades; her plan is the genre's comeback set-up; Mochiko's silence is realism and respect (principal) | Principal's running notes | For: i0069–i0074, i0080–i0082, i0097–i0098, i0114–i0115, i0135, i0140, i0143. | FXK-V09-C001 | V09's governing thesis: the debt is separated from the desire that produced it — the debt closed through institutions and family (bankruptcy "一択", the confession, the father's 300万 offered for the costs, a lawyer, a discharge under her name; the 2014 tax paid "地道に" with wages the father manages), the desire owned rather than cured (innate greed named, the mother's failure admitted as a pretext, "楽しかった自分がいる", "似てるけど違う", the vow refused, "相場が大好きだから", "ただいま", "支配して コントロールしてみせる"); 共生 as coexistence conducted openly ("堂々と"), made possible by Mochiko's non-denial and the father's refusal of anger with his opposition kept; 2017 tests it only by displacement (principal) | Principal's page record and STATE; F01–F10; MAIN_EXIT | i0017, i0031–i0033, i0058, i0061, i0063, i0069–i0084, i0098, i0104–i0107, i0119–i0130, i0152, i0154 | NEW; EVAL on OBS HIGH; design EVAL MOD-HIGH (a live alternative reading of 共生: FXK-V09-C075); V09 reading §D.1 |
| FXK-V09-C002 | Competing thesis: a restoration comedy that dissolves V08's crisis faster than its premises allow — the debt vanishes off-page between two captions with no creditor or figure; a parent's money is offered; every feared consequence becomes relief; the friends' concern becomes a gag; Kurumi, the protagonist, is decentred by a new cast; "共生" as a licence to keep the market engine without showing the cost, the risk moved onto Kizuna and Mebuki (principal) | Principal's page record | For: i0048, i0098, i0104–i0105, i0091–i0097, i0122, i0133–i0158. | FXK-V10-C001 | V10's governing thesis: the market moves from Kurumi's body to her workplace; the narrator sows "欲望の種" in four women and the volume watches three uses of money for selfhood — Kizuna's net worth as existence ("自分の存在を確かめる", "凡人止まり"), Nana's shares as fandom rank ("高次元の推し活", "私のメイくん"), Inago's inherited suspicion ("騙された いなごは 喰われて おしまいさ") — while Kurumi, the only one at that table who has lived the outcome, conceals its scale, rehearses the return as a question and is left asking the price of another's bet; the founding mechanisms (early win, ceiling call, liquidation at the top; a contrarian short against the trend) are re-staged on others (principal) | Principal's page record and STATE; F01–F05; MAIN_EXIT | i0033–i0035, i0040, i0042–i0050, i0052–i0056, i0068–i0076, i0100–i0101, i0109–i0110, i0121–i0124, i0140–i0143, i0146, i0152–i0156 | NEW; EVAL on OBS HIGH; design EVAL MOD-HIGH; V10 reading §D.1 |

For prospects preserve predictions, confidence, disconfirming signals and actual exposure before resolution. Attribute independent tests separately. For revisions use `PRESERVE`, `STRENGTHEN`, `REVISE`, `DOWNGRADE`, `REJECT` or `OPEN`. Keep the historical argument and use a new linked entry for a material change.

Ledger version 1.16 (2026-11-23; FXK-SYN-20261123-V01-V10, G4 close-out): rows FXK-SYN-C029–C032 (the integrated synthesis's transitions P-1 to P-4, written by the principal as a late G3 transaction) and FXK-SYN-C033–C034 (dated corrections of FXK-SYN-C002 and FXK-V07-C026) are appended to the table above, and a dated correction note is appended to the 高根やす子 entry below; no earlier row, note or explanatory paragraph changes. The transaction is recorded in the [semantic validation audit](audits/FX_KURUMI_V01_V10_SEMANTIC_VALIDATION.md), section 11.

## Person-level qualification

Subjects assessed through V10 (FXK-JP-V01-W01, FXK-JP-V02-W01, FXK-JP-V03-W01, FXK-JP-V04-W01, FXK-JP-V05-W01, FXK-JP-V06-W01, FXK-JP-V07-W01, FXK-JP-V08-W01, FXK-JP-V09-W01 and FXK-JP-V10-W01) are listed after this paragraph. Record each eligible person, source and horizon, distinct habits, ordinary choices, Japanese speech, cognition under differing conditions, directional ties, counterexamples, evidence sufficiency and reassessment threshold. Link a completed monograph and its separate self-containment and fidelity decisions once accepted.

- **福賀くるみ (Fukuga Kurumi)**: source W01 V01; horizon ages 15 (2008–09) and 20 (Feb–May 2014). Evidence
  sufficiency: HIGH; she is on nearly every main page.
  - Distinct habits: compulsive chart-checking (commute, work, walk home); war and shōnen self-framing; frugality
    (umbrella); studying; cheerful performance to her father.
  - Ordinary choices: a commuting university for her father's sake; 特待生; part-time convenience-store work; WcDon
    lunches; a small drink in the student lounge; cardigans and ribbon ties; the "Live & Let Die" mug; Mochiko saved in
    her phone as hiragana "もちこ".
  - Speech: first person 私; casual じゃん/もん/よね; service register at work (いらっしゃいませー); masculine-leaning
    self-commands under strain (いくぞ, 行けっ, 上がれ); coarse under anger (なってンだっけ, ふざけんな); childlike under
    panic (やだやだ, やめてよぉ); polite emphasis (いりません).
  - Cognition under differing conditions: strategic and risk-aware when calm (i0042–i0045, i0064–i0066); euphoric
    after gains (i0049, i0073); rationalizing under moderate loss (i0089–i0097); sober flashes mid-crisis ("今はまだ
    耐えただけ…", i0122); felt incapacity under loss of family money (i0131, i0135); globally self-condemning at the
    nadir (i0139, i0141).
  - Directional ties: mother (grief, guilt, identification); father (love, concealment, unconsented use of his
    savings); Mochiko (trust, care for her as a lender, one lie, gratitude); Yūko (friend, offstage).
  - Counterexamples: refusals of all-in, 100枚 and the loan, partly out of worry for the lender ("困るのは萌智子でしょ?",
    i0066); a fast exit in Trade 1; a five-year deferral to legal age.
  - Reassessment threshold: any V02 scene of restitution or disclosure to the father; her interpretation of the win;
    whether she adopts stop orders or sizes up.
  - **V02 update** (FXK-JP-V02-W01; horizon late May – 15 July 2014, age 20). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH.
    - Distinct habits: still works the convenience-store job (i0012); reads savings books at night (i0084); a
      bookshop habit and a book-buying binge topped by "占い師のなり方" as a coping plan (i0094–i0096); keeps her
      first-grade "くるみのへや" door sign (i0114); watches live charts while "retired" (i0092, i0116); minimal assent
      under pressure ("…うん", "う…ん", "え…うん"; i0038, i0055, i0087).
    - Ordinary choices: a tote bag lettered "Y・H・V・H"; a hairclip; a high school spent only studying (i0093); a
      childhood drawing habit and the dream of being a kindergarten teacher (i0111–i0112); pachinko tried once,
      disliked, and regretted as books not bought (i0098–i0099).
    - Speech: 私; childish wheedling to her father while confessing (i0029); flustered refusals; a menacing teacher
      mode (i0065); soft, unrepeated requests (i0115); "戦う" for trading (i0017, i0131, i0140); address 萌智子,
      芽吹ちゃん, やす子ちゃん.
    - Cognition under differing conditions: outcome justification when cornered (i0029); luck attribution under her
      father's plea and in private (i0033, i0041, i0087–i0088, i0101); craving at others' gains (i0072, i0086);
      minimizing at thresholds ("様子見程度", "ちょろっと", "大人の遊び"; i0092, i0097, i0099); self-licensing after
      one open gain (i0129–i0131); a contrarian rule drawn from Mebuki's misses (i0141); a mild public account of the
      relapse (i0140) after a private vow of self-destruction (i0132).
    - Directional ties: father (concealment broken; assent without commitment; his worry acknowledged and
      overridden, i0130); Mochiko (doubt, then a bantering alliance of mutual use, i0145); Mebuki (reluctant adviser,
      then silent user of her views, i0141); Yasuko (kind host, i0150–i0152); mother (memory, i0111–i0112).
    - Counterexamples: structural de-risking (i0017); intent to file tax (i0018); several resisted impulses (i0092,
      i0096, i0099, i0116); an attempt to end the advising (i0115); "誰か私を止めて…!" (i0109).
    - Money: renewed trading with no pair, size, entry or stop shown; domestic balance 2,100,000 (deposit undated)
      → 2,656,690 (i0102, i0158).
    - Reassessment threshold: the first trade shown with pair and size; any loss; any disclosure to the father or to
      Mebuki; whether the narrator's "呪い" pays off.
  - **V03 update** (FXK-JP-V03-W01; horizon late July – late October 2014, age 20). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH overall;
    MODERATE for V03 alone (about 25 pages; she is offstage for most of Mebuki's story).
    - Distinct habits: trades during class on her phone (i0055); shares positions with Mochiko by phone and in lectures
      (i0010–i0011, i0053–i0055); draws mascot doodles; hand-makes rule sheets with hearts (i0086); announces orders as
      "○○枚発射" (i0138).

      does not know コミチア (i0082).
    - Speech: 私; cheerful casual with hearts; playful politeness to the group; minimizing ("軽く30枚くらい", "レバ20倍は低レバ");
      macro jargon (地政学リスク, QE3, ドル安調整; i0122); address 萌智子, 芽吹ちゃん, やす子ちゃん.
    - Cognition under differing conditions: forgone gain → "次はもっと欲張ろう" and a 2500万 target (i0053–i0054); open gain →
      regret at not sizing up (i0056); large win → "今回は欲張っちゃった" (i0091) then a self-limiting 1,000,000 withdrawal
      "無茶して失敗しないように" (i0124); reasoned bullish case (i0121–i0122).
    - Directional ties: Mochiko (open trust; she knew of Mochiko's earlier gains yet believes the false −10%, i0090;
      unaware of Mochiko's lies, of her contempt and of her disclosure of how Kurumi won in V01, framed as "借金");
      Mebuki (asks Mochiko whether Mebuki has been in touch, i0057; concern about school, i0103 by chat icon, i0120; her July
      trade kept quiet under the contest frame and a stay-out answer on the pound without a reason, i0024–i0026; never
      learns of Mebuki's position or plight); Yasuko (curious, inclusive, i0082–i0085); father (mentioned only, i0080).
    - Counterexamples: the withdrawal and tax reserve (i0124); no overseas account shown (labelled boxes 国内口座).
    - Money: EUR/USD short 40枚 ("43万円" by Mebuki's report); USD/JPY 30枚 at 104.221 realized ≈+1,562,700 (exit ≈109.43,
      CALC); 4,611,715 (73.5%増) → withdrawal → 3,611,715; USD/JPY 60枚 at 106.789, +456,600 open (i0154). Every trade she closes is profitable; no loss, no stop.
    - Reassessment threshold: her first depicted loss and whether she holds it (V01 C010 still untested); learning of Mebuki's
      plight or of Mochiko's lies; any present-time scene with her father.
  - **V04 update** (FXK-JP-V04-W01; horizon 31 October – 5 November 2014, age 20). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH overall; MODERATE
    for V04 alone (about 30 pages; offstage after i0115).
    - Distinct habits: announces orders as battle commands ("全力 買い(ロング)…!", "120枚発射…!!", i0006); laughs behind her door at a

      trading, she retracts: "あ…嘘だよ! 世間体だってあるし! FXは勝ち逃げする予定だし!" (i0036); cooks curry for her father and eats alone
      when he is late (i0115); a two-storey apartment block with an external walkway (i0065); her room with bookshelves, PC desk,
      full-length mirror and a framed family photograph (i0065, i0072); her contact with Mebuki has thinned (i0051).
    - Speech: 私; hearts and "えへ♡"; gentle address to a junior ("あのね 芽吹ちゃん", i0068); firmness without leaving the casual

      i0076); hedged self-assessment ("冷静な判断… だと思う…", i0115).

      unease ("不気味", "要警戒") and a refusal to add "根拠がなさすぎる" (i0112–i0115); a friend's crisis → care with a limit (food money),
      refusal on protective grounds (i0067–i0072), then, alone, her father's maxim (i0074) and self-doubt ("最初からFXを教えた…?",
      i0075); accused of hypocrisy → "運がよかっただけ…で…" (i0073).

    - Counterexamples: the declined second add (i0115) against the first (i0006); "勝ち逃げ" said (i0036), nothing realized.
    - Money: 180枚 USD/JPY long at 110.271 (120枚 added at 112.012); +7,812,000 open at 114.611 (CALC exact); balance 3,611,715
      unchanged; no stop; CALC ≈29.9× at the add, a tension with a 25× maximum printed only for Yasuko's account.
    - Disclosure: tells Mochiko and Yasuko that her mother killed herself because of FX (i0054) — first in the corpus; the causal link is

    - Distinct habits: reads the chart after the liquidation and clicks again and again at a rejected order she does not understand (i0022–i0023); fairy-tale self-narration
      ("私はスイスに殺されちゃいましたとさ", i0053); brisk self-commands over grave acts ("さ…先に 身辺整理 するか!", i0050); switches off and
      refuses what she cannot face (phone, i0056; her father's call, i0036); her body dissents before her words (trembling pen, vomiting, halted feet, a fist against a utility pole; i0066, i0078, i0084, i0088).
    - Ordinary choices: family flat unit 「201」 「福賀」 (i0115); room sign 「くるみのへや」; strawberry pyjamas and bedding (i0148–i0158);
      keeps childhood things — 「さんすうセット」 「1-1 ふくがくるみ」, play money, a world map, 「作文ノート」 「4-2」, textbooks, a diploma
      (i0051–i0054); sorts rubbish by the schedule (i0044); 「すてきなレターセット」 (i0065); remembers the pool, watermelon, 優子's game,
      meals with her father, 山口さん's recipes (i0052–i0053, i0072, i0079, i0085); in crisis, no food, no sleep, no bath for days (i0130, i0145).
    - Speech: 私; "萌智子", "芽吹ちゃん", "優子ちゃん", "お父さん", "お母さん"; polite stock phrases to strangers ("だ… 大丈夫です", i0082); the
      letter in formal です/ます (i0067–i0075); trading cries ("発射ッ!!!", "りカく!!!"); "ユロスイ"; 傍点 on 自己責任 (i0130); childlike "やだ…"
      (i0037), "…たくない" (i0138); a lethal sentence in a gentle request form ("もう死ぬから 出ていって?", i0123); Mochiko's "たられば" taken up as her own (i0155).
    - Cognition under differing conditions: two wins → "私の実力", "勝ち確" on a dip-buy rule, size 300 → 600 → 1000 (i0008–i0015); after the liquidation, a rejected order she does not understand (i0023); then an exact, self-condemning reckoning (tax 5500万 on a hypothetical 1億; 予納金 150万; 2014 tax ≈3,807,983 undischargeable;
      i0030–i0034); responsibility converted into a duty to die via 相続放棄 (i0042–i0047); a plan pursued through a 遺書, a search, purchases
      (i0048–i0083), with fear and delay (i0067, i0078, i0084); blame of Mochiko then apology (i0121–i0123); under Mochiko's question, "死にたく
      ないよ!" while calling that wish "無責任" (i0139); a forward question, "これからどうしよう…" (i0155).
    - Directional ties: her father (love, guilt, fear of contempt, concealment; a confession left for after death, i0036–i0037, i0066–i0076);
      Kozue (re-enactment: her last note's words 「すみませんでした」 on a sheet, i0042, recalled or rewritten; "私も 責任 取らなきゃ…" echoing Kozue's V01 "これは私の責任なんだから"; "待っててね… お母さん…", i0065; the V01 outburst re-read,
      i0086); Mochiko (blame, apology, disclosure, clinging trust, sleep holding her hand; i0121–i0158); 優子 and 山口さん (warm memory, no
      contact); strangers (the cashier; "社会は滞りなく回っていく", i0046; "処理してくれる人", i0081).
    - Counterexamples: lucid reasoning, not blindness (i0030–i0034); a faint smile for schoolchildren (i0045); care for her father and for
      a cleaner even inside the plan (i0047, i0081); delays and a detour (i0084–i0090); the wish to live (i0139); thanks (i0151); sleep (i0158).
    - Money: overseas account +1,000,000 (i0003; INF MOD-HIGH the tax reserve) → 2,545,501 (i0009) → 4,519,175 (i0012) → 0円 by 強制ロスカット (i0020); main account
      −101,534,644 (V07; unprinted in V08; "1億円を超える借金", i0047); 2014 tax ≈3,807,983 (i0034); bank balance after the deposit unprinted.
    - Reassessment threshold: whether the crisis has ended or only paused; what she accepts from Mochiko; telling her father; the debt path
      and the tax; any return to trading.
  - **V09 update** (FXK-JP-V09-W01; horizon from the day after V08's night 3 (undated, early 2015) through early February 2015 and
    autumn 2015 to Monday 3 April 2017; 23 in April 2017, i0152). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH (most main pages of 第41–44話; i0151–i0158).
    - Distinct habits: names her own greed and tests it against others (i0031, i0112); "爆益" as a signature word (i0023, i0103); trader

      five times (i0109, i0122); 「くるみのへや」 (i0123); graduation thesis "爆益を狙った場合における投資行動の一考察" (i0103); convenience-store

      "ビンタされちゃう" (i0040); 2017 "いなごちゃん", "きずなさん" (i0152, i0157).

      states a future in decades (i0078); "相場が大好きだから" (i0121); resolves to "支配して コントロールしてみせる" (i0128).
    - Directional ties: father (concealment → confession, i0040 → i0063; affection, accepted control of her wages, i0107; apology for his
      journeys, i0123); Kozue (apology, separation, i0061); Mochiko (accepts her help and offer by outcome; clerk in 2017); Yasuko and

    - Money: bankrupt and discharged (平成27年11月, i0104); the father's 300万 offered for the costs and accepted in thanks, its use not shown (i0074–i0075); 2014 tax in instalments (i0106);
      wages managed by the father (i0107); no trade, account or position shown in V09.

  - **V10 update** (FXK-JP-V10-W01; horizon Monday (INF 3 April 2017) to Monday 24 April 2017; 23 per the V09 name box). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH

      amount and the bankruptcy (i0042–i0045; an inset reprises her V08 reckoning, 「3,807,983」, "自己破産出来たとしても"); revalues her past ten million ("失ってから気づいた", i0048) and her

    - Counterexamples: lets Kizuna's "1億円負けなんてありえない" (i0035) and "欲張ったら" (i0045) stand; endorses Kizuna's カリフラワー idea (i0154).
    - Money: 22,963円 (CAP, i0043); 2014 national tax still partly unpaid (amount not printed; the V08 figure reprised in an inset, i0045); no account, order, position or screen of hers in V10; pay not stated.
      INF MOD-HIGH "株はまだまだ勉強中だし!" (i0146).

- **小金萌智子 (Kogane Mochiko)**: source W01 V01; horizon May 2014 (i0058–i0162). Evidence sufficiency:
  MODERATE-HIGH (about 30 pages; rich private thought).
  - Distinct habits: watching markets instead of attending class; a cat-ear/flower phone case; coffee from a "珈琲"
    cup in the lounge; a high-rise flat with a four-monitor rig; a white cat stroked while she talks.
  - Speech: 私; blunt casual (クソみたいな相場, わからん, ワンチャン); coarser in thought (知らねー, 下手クソかよ,
    死んどけや); brief public congratulations (さすがにここは反発するかぁ… おめでと; よかったね…).
  - Cognition: a disciplined plan (wait; sell only if 96 is reached; i0070) whose trigger never came (i0092); an
    accurate risk reading of Kurumi (i0155–i0156); two wrong outcome calls (i0154, i0157).
  - Directional ties: Kurumi (public warmth and help in effect; private contempt, "観察", enjoyment of anticipated
    ruin evidenced only by her private lines at i0155–i0159); Mebuki (incredulity).
  - Counterexamples to malice: check-in call, liquidation question, candour about her contrary preference, urging
    that led Kurumi to look, returned embrace.
  - Not established: manipulation for gain; any position that would gain from Kurumi's loss; childhood context.
  - Reassessment threshold: any act that causes or profits from a friend's loss, or any act of costly help; any
    surfacing of her private view to Kurumi.
  - **V02 update** (FXK-JP-V02-W01; horizon late May – 15 July 2014). Evidence sufficiency: MODERATE-HIGH.
    - Distinct habits: reads investor blogs for amusement and did "nothing" on a weekday (i0008); feeds a stray white
      cat while denying she keeps it (i0006); a cat-face phone case (i0047); lectures (i0055).
    - Speech: 私 / 私ら; plain and teasing aloud; one "くるみちゃん" at the flattery peak (i0040); coarse in thought
      ("めんどくさ…", "誰が踏むかよ…", "反吐が出るほど甘いな…"); a dare in banter ("いいよ… やってみな…?", i0145).
    - Cognition: a results-only worldview ("結果を出した奴だけがすごい", i0151); traders as "敵同士" (i0143); a passive
      contest plan expecting the others to drop out (thought, i0160); a quick read of Yasuko's jargon (i0156).
    - Directional ties: Kurumi (steering back into FX through a proxy role, its aim undisclosed until "嬉しい" and
      "半分正解", i0040–i0055, i0144–i0145); Mebuki (500× and "一発" advice, i0007, i0059; a brief "うん よかったね" to
      her win reports, i0142; her own account domestic, i0158); あふぃちゃん (amusement at her losses and a deceptive
      comment urging her not to retire, i0010–i0011); Yasuko (contempt for her dream, delight in her drawing,
      suspicion of her past, i0151–i0156).
    - Counterexamples to malice: the cat; helping Mebuki open an account; "勉強したら?" (i0010); "きゅんっ", "…悪くない",
      "もっと描け" (i0152); candour about half her motive (i0145).
    - Capital: domestic 12,014,110円 (i0158). No trade shown.
    - Not established: any position that gains from a friend's loss; enjoyment of Kurumi's or Mebuki's loss (none
      occurs); childhood context; how she knows Kurumi trades "前より" (she knew of the forecasting from Mebuki's
      calls, i0142).
    - Reassessment threshold: any act that profits from or enjoys a friend's loss; the other "half" of her motive;
      whether she links Yasuko to あふぃちゃん.
  - **V03 update** (FXK-JP-V03-W01; horizon late July – late October 2014). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH (decisive acts and rich

    - Distinct habits: a white cat on her lap during calls (i0146); business reading ("会社経営の仕組み", i0146) and bookstore

      "クソポジ", "売り豚", "焼豚", "めんどくさ…"); trader slang (ユロル, 養分, 膿が出尽くして, 上値が軽い, 炭鉱のカナリア); "くるみ"
      to Kurumi, "くるみちゃん" of Kurumi to others (i0093, i0102–i0104), "芽吹ちゃん".
    - Cognition: hedged public macro view later named by the narrator as the majority error (i0123, i0161); correct
      structural read of the October chart (i0146); risk control by a stop (i0152); strategic deception for informational
      advantage (canary, i0149–i0150).
    - Directional ties: Mebuki (knows the money is tuition, i0099; hold-supporting advice with the parents as backstop,
      i0100–i0101; a knowingly false assent, i0126; a lie about her own position in answer to Mebuki's direct question,
      after hearing her hedged willingness to exit, i0147–i0148; private relish and
      contempt, i0098–i0099, i0126, i0147, i0151); Kurumi (private exasperation and respect, i0091, i0126; nudges toward
      leverage and size, i0054, i0057; a false loss report, i0090; disclosure of her inference that Kurumi won with borrowed
      money, framed as gambling, i0104–i0105); Yasuko (a marketing judgement, i0082).
    - Counterexamples to malice: "なぁんだ… まともな親いるじゃん…" (i0096); "まぁまぁ 落ちついて" (i0099); she takes Mebuki's call
      (i0146); wants to forfeit rather than win (i0093).
    - Money: 13,302,630 on 9/19 (+10.7%; claimed −10%); USD/JPY 50枚 long at 106.832 (106.382 at i0154, transposition) with a
      stop typed in (level unshown); +359,000 open in late October (i0155).
    - Established now: a position that gains from a friend's loss, held while urging that friend to stay opposite (i0148);
      enjoyment in private register (i0099, i0151). Not established: a wish to hurt as an end; childhood history (family
      distance only).
    - Reassessment threshold: exposure of either lie; her reaction to Mebuki's capitulation ("the canary stops singing");
      any costly help; any surfacing of her private view to Kurumi.
  - **V04 update** (FXK-JP-V04-W01; horizon 31 October – a few days after 5 November 2014). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH (decisive acts,

    - Directional ties: Mebuki (creditor at 109.5%, IOU 2,000,000; affirmed the shared-short lie, i0087; answered her first exit with a

    - Owner objective C: understands Mebuki's vulnerabilities (tuition, mother, fear of exposure); exploits them in lending (INF HIGH);

  - **Correction (2026-11-23; FXK-SYN-20261123-V01-V10, principal):** the V09 and V10 updates above stand as written and are read
    with this note. (a) V10 update, "shows she funded an account again after the 2013 liquidation": INFERENCE HIGH, not shown; the pages
    print the 2013 liquidation (V10 i0076) and the 2014 balance of 76,573円 (V02 i0159), not whether the 2014 account is a new one
    ([reconciliation audit](audits/FX_KURUMI_SOURCE_COMPLETION_AND_LONGITUDINAL_RECONCILIATION.md) §4.6). (b) V09 update, "あたし / ウチ": ウチ in
    "なんでウチがタコパ会場になってんだよっ!!" (i0109) names her home, the party's venue, and is not a first-person form; "まぁうちは 別に
    いいけどよぉ" on the same page reads either way, so her V09 first person is あたし. (c) V09 update, "construction at home (i0148)": the
    construction is next door, "家だと隣の工事がうるさくてよぉ〜" (i0148; V09 reading §H.4). V09 i0109 and i0148 re-opened; source: the FXK-P008
    review ([semantic validation audit](audits/FX_KURUMI_V01_V10_SEMANTIC_VALIDATION.md) §9 item 8, §11).

    この子は…" (i0160–i0161). Kurumi now frames her loss as timing (i0153) and her 2000万 as only "お母さんの負け額" (i0154).

  i0029, i0137; a leopard-coat man, i0111); Inago's past interviewers (i0138); villagers and children (i0133). The winged girl
  (Kurumi's personified greed; i0003–i0009, i0030–i0031, i0054, i0124–i0130) is a figure of Kurumi's (INF MOD-HIGH the accepted V02 tempter, FXK-V09-C074), recorded under her entry, not a
  person. None named; none eligible. 毒島☆寿美 does not appear in V09.
- Apparatus and extras in V09 (i0001–i0002, i0045–i0046, i0099–i0100, i0131–i0132, i0159–i0171: covers, flap, jacket blurb and collage,
  separators, design roughs, the writer's afterword, the AU bonus comic, the writer's essay, colophon, vendor page) are paratext and are
  not used in these notes.
- **Minor figures (V10)** (new entry): Inago's mother (unnamed; flashback, i0034; a farming mother with a sickle; "儲け話には絶対乗っちゃいけないよ?");
  Nana's mother (unnamed; INF MOD-HIGH; glasses, apron; "社会人面するなら家にお金入れるくらいしたら?", "おもちゃばっか買ってるくせに", i0056–i0057);
  Yasuko's editor (unnamed; a man in a leopard-print suit in a gag inset, i0086; whether he is the V05 毒島☆寿美 is not stated); fellow fans named by
  screen-name only on Nana's screen (「♪みーん@綺羅メイ推し♪」, 「ゆり」, 「ぽんぽこ」, 「ゆた」, 「ゆひゃら」, 「ゆんちゃ」; i0083–i0084, i0100); the
  restaurant waiter (i0052); students at the 2013 job fair (i0066); staff at 講報堂 (i0118); caricatured politicians in the Brexit exposition (i0115–i0116;
  not identified). 綺羅メイ and the other characters of 「ダンスのぷりんすさまっ!」 (i0148) are in-world media characters, not persons; recorded because
  Nana's decisions are framed through メイ. The winged Kurumi of the i0033 vision is a figure of Kurumi's (recorded under her entry), not a person. None
  eligible.
- Apparatus and extras in V10 (i0001–i0002, i0157–i0171: covers, flap, jacket blurb and collage, separator, artist-side bonus manga, a pre-serialization
  drawing sample, the writer's afterword, the under-jacket essay and manga, colophon, vendor page) are paratext and are not used in these notes.
- Monographs: none completed or linked.

### V01–V10 qualification (FXK-SYN-20261123-V01-V10)

Current subject IDs, names and outcomes at the V01–V10 boundary (gate G2, 2026-11-23; outcomes and results of the eight monograph subjects updated at gate G4, 2026-11-23, ledger version 1.15); the arguments, name forms, gaps and review triggers are in the [character qualification audit](audits/FX_KURUMI_CHARACTER_QUALIFICATION.md). The entries above remain the dated evidence; this table states the current outcome. Gate G4 recorded the self-containment and fidelity results separately, each linked to its record in the [semantic validation audit](audits/FX_KURUMI_V01_V10_SEMANTIC_VALIDATION.md); a subject is COMPLETE_AT_BOUNDARY only when both pass, and its published monograph is linked.
